The canonical PDZ-binding motif typically ends at the very last residue of the protein. The consensus for PDZ-binding motifs is usually [S/T]-X-[V/I/L]-COOH at the C-terminus.

Looking at the options, the motif that includes the very last residue (position -1) would be D. Residues 521–523 (TRM).

PDZ-binding motifs are characteristically located at the extreme C-terminus of the protein. The motif &quot;TRM&quot; at the very end of the protein would be the most likely PDZ-binding motif, as PDZ-binding motifs need to interact with the binding pocket of PDZ domains, which accommodates the free C-terminal carboxylate group.
